## Provides

### PRD-01 Rendering Core / Scene Graph / Color / HDR / PBR

Extraction note: (docs/project/aura3d-quality-rebuild/PRD-01-rendering-core-color-hdr-pbr.md). Line refs checked with rg/sed at HEAD 8a878fb3. Nearly all match the code. One mismatch: createProductionRuntimeRendererInput starts at index.ts:13842, not the 13871 the PRD cites (the call sites at 13976 and 13980 are correct). Not yet present (PRD creates them): packages/rendering/src/{program,output,resources}/, BlendModes.ts, ResolutionGovernor.ts, packages/engine/src/agent-api/{sceneGraph,color}.ts, tools/shader-lint/. Mostly a provider PRD. It hard-blocks PRD 02 (generator Phase 3), PRD 03 (OutputPass/HDR Phase 4), PRD 04 (brdf chunk Phase 3), PRD 06 (morph textures and skinned programs), PRD 07 (blend modes Phase 2) and PRD 10 (scene graph and fog slot).
- Generator feature record: interface ProgramFeatures { pass: 'forward'|'depth'|'distance'|'velocity'; target: 'glsl300es'|'wgsl'; lighting: 'lit'|'unlit'; maps: { baseColor?, normal?, metallicRoughness?, occlusion?, emissive?: TextureSlotFeature }; extensions: readonly MaterialExtensionFeature[] /* members defined by PRD 04 */; alphaMode: 'opaque'|'mask'|'blend'; doubleSided: boolean; vertexColors: boolean; flatShading: boolean; skinning?: { influences: 4|8; palette: 'uniform'|'texture' }; morph?: { targetBucket: 4|8|16|32; normals: boolean; tangents: boolean }; instancing?: { color: boolean }; lights: { dir, point, spot: 0|1|2|4|8; rect: 0|1|2|4; clustered: boolean; hemisphere: boolean }; shadows: ShadowReceiveFeature /* PRD 02 */; environment: 'none'|'pmrem-cube'|'equirect' /* PRD 02 */; fog: 'none'|'linear'|'exp2'|'height' /* PRD 10 may extend */; diffuseModel: 'lambert'|'burley'; specularAntialiasing: boolean; backgroundCoverage: boolean } (packages/rendering/src/program/ProgramFeatures.ts); interface TextureSlotFeature { uvSet: 0|1; transform: boolean }
- function computeProgramKey(features: ProgramFeatures): string (program/ProgramKey.ts)
- interface GeneratedProgram { key: string; vertex: string; fragment: string; defines: Readonly<Record<string, string|number|true>> }; function generateProgram(features: ProgramFeatures): GeneratedProgram (program/ProgramGenerator.ts). Chunks in program/chunks/{common,colorspace,brdf,lights_pars,lights_fragment,shadow_pars,ibl_pars,skinning,morph,instancing,normal,material,fog,depth}.glsl.ts: hook points for PRD 02, 04 and 10. Depth programs via pass:'depth' are the hook PRD 02 needs for skinned and instanced shadows.
- interface ProgramHandle { key: string; status: 'pending'|'ready'|'failed'; program?: RenderShaderProgram; error?: string }; class ProgramCache { constructor(device: RenderDevice, options?: { parallelCompile?: boolean }); acquire(f: ProgramFeatures): ProgramHandle; precompile(list: readonly ProgramFeatures[]): Promise<void>; stats(): { compiled: number; pending: number; failed: number; compileMsTotal: number }; dispose(): void }; ProgramWarmup.precompile(scene) awaited by app.ready()
- abstract Material.programFeatures(context: MaterialFeatureContext): Omit<ProgramFeatures, 'lights'|'shadows'|'environment'|'fog'|'pass'|'target'|'backgroundCoverage'>
- brdf GLSL chunk: struct PhysicalMaterial { diffuseContribution, baseColor, metallic, roughness, specularColor, specularColorBlended, specularF90, ior, specularIntensity }; a3dMakeMaterial(...); F_Schlick, V_GGX_SmithCorrelated, D_GGX, a3dDFG(roughness, dotNV), a3dDirectSpecular (r185 BRDF_GGX_Multiscatter), a3dDirectLight; renderer-owned u_dfgLut (r185 16x16 RG16F DFG LUT in BRDFLut.ts). Base lobe for PRD 04 extensions.
- UBO layout std140 AuraFrame (binding 0: u_view, u_projection, u_viewProjection, u_prevViewProjection, u_cameraPositionNear, u_resolutionFarTime, u_exposureFlags) and AuraLights (binding 1); resources/UniformBlock.ts std140 packer. Gives post passes real near/far (PRD 03).
- OutputPass: interface OutputPassOptions { toneMapping: AuraToneMappingOperatorLike; exposure: number; dithering: boolean; backgroundCoverage: boolean }; class OutputPass { constructor(device: RenderDevice); execute(input: RenderTarget, coverage: RenderTarget|null, options: OutputPassOptions, output: RenderTarget|'canvas'): void }; DEFAULT_TONE_MAPPING constant; output/ToneMappingOperators.glsl.ts (none/linear/reinhard/aces/agx r185/Khronos neutral). PRD 03 must not add another tone mapper.
- HDR post contract: every pass between MSAA resolve and OutputPass declares space:'linear-hdr' (RGBA16F); display-space passes before OutputPass are rejected at plan time; non-GPU passes are rejected with POSTPROCESS_PASS_NOT_GPU:<name> (RendererPostprocessPlan.ts). HDR target probe 'rgba16f'→'r11f_g11f_b10f'→'rgba8' (degraded).
- Blend modes: type BlendFactor = 'zero'|'one'|'src-color'|'one-minus-src-color'|'src-alpha'|'one-minus-src-alpha'|'dst-color'|'one-minus-dst-color'|'dst-alpha'|'one-minus-dst-alpha'; type BlendEquation = 'add'|'subtract'|'reverse-subtract'|'min'|'max'; interface BlendComponent { equation; src; dst }; type BlendMode = 'opaque'|'alpha'|'premultiplied'|'additive'|'multiply'|{ kind:'custom'; color: BlendComponent; alpha: BlendComponent }; type DepthCompare = 'never'|'less'|'equal'|'less-equal'|'greater'|'not-equal'|'greater-equal'|'always'; RenderState.blend / RenderCommandState.blend: BlendMode; function renderStateKey(state: RenderCommandState): number. Engine: type AuraBlendMode = 'opaque'|'alpha'|'premultiplied'|'additive'|'multiply'; AuraMaterialSpec.blend?, depthWrite?, ior? (reaches F0), specularIntensity?, specularColor?. Consumed by PRD 07 (VFX additive HDR accumulation), PRD 04 and PRD 11 (WebGPU conformance).
- Scene graph (packages/engine/src/agent-api/sceneGraph.ts): composeWorldMatrix(parent: Float32Array, local: AuraTransformSpec, out: Float32Array): Float32Array; decomposeMatrix(m: Float32Array): AuraWorldTransform; eulerToQuaternion(euler: AuraVec3, order: AuraEulerOrder): readonly [number, number, number, number]; type AuraEulerOrder = 'XYZ'|'XZY'|'YXZ'|'YZX'|'ZXY'|'ZYX'; AuraTransformSpec gains rotationOrder? (default 'ZYX'), quaternion?, lookAt resolved in world space; interface AuraWorldTransform { matrix: Float32Array; position: AuraVec3; quaternion: readonly [number, number, number, number]; scale: AuraVec3; sheared: boolean }; builder .rotate(x,y,z,order?) and .quaternion(x,y,z,w); per-node version and dirty flag; SCENE_GRAPH_SHEAR warning. Consumed by PRD 08 (world lookAt), 09 (game kits as groups), 10 and 15 (three-compat order mapping).
- Primitive geometry: interface AuraPrimitiveTessellation { widthSegments?, heightSegments?, depthSegments?, radialSegments?, tubularSegments?, capSegments?, openEnded?, radiusTop?, radiusBottom?, tube? }; AuraPrimitiveOptions.tessellation?; @aura3d/rendering Geometry.torus(o?: TorusGeometryOptions), Geometry.box(o?: BoxGeometryOptions), Geometry.litPlane(o?: PlaneGeometryOptions), extended Geometry.cylinder({ radiusTop, radiusBottom, heightSegments, capped }), Geometry.capsule({ ellipticity }), uvSphere textured default. All emit position/normal/uv0/tangent, with Uint32 indices above 65,535 vertices. Shared geometry cache keyed by (primitive, params). Consumed by PRD 10 and 06.
- class InstanceBuffer { constructor(device: RenderDevice, capacity: number, options?: { colors?: boolean }); readonly count: number; readonly version: number; setMatrices(m: Float32Array, count?: number): void; setColors(c: Float32Array): void; bind(): { matrixBuffer: RenderBuffer; colorBuffer?: RenderBuffer }; dispose(): void } (resources/InstanceBuffer.ts). Instancing order world·instance·geometry with u_modelMatrix and u_geometryMatrix; fixes size scaling instance translation. Consumed by PRD 07, 10 and 09.
- class MorphTargetTexture { static fromGeometry(device: RenderDevice, geometry: Geometry, targets: readonly MorphTarget[]): MorphTargetTexture; readonly targetCount: number; readonly hasNormals: boolean; readonly hasTangents: boolean; dispose(): void }; wrapped RGBA16F TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY morph layout (width ≤ 1024), weight buckets {4,8,16,32}, MORPH_TARGETS_TRUNCATED warning. Consumed by PRD 06 (faces/visemes).
- interface ResolutionGovernorOptions { targetFrameMs: number; minRenderScale: number; maxRenderScale: number; hysteresisDownFrames?: number; hysteresisUpFrames?: number }; class ResolutionGovernor { constructor(o: ResolutionGovernorOptions); sample(frameMs: number, gpuMs?: number): number; reset(): void }. The only resolution state machine; createPerformanceGovernor and setPerformanceQuality drive it. Consumed by PRD 11 tiers.
- Engine renderer options: type AuraQualityTier = 'low'|'medium'|'high'|'ultra'|'auto'; type AuraToneMappingOperator = 'none'|'linear'|'reinhard'|'aces'|'agx'|'neutral'; interface AuraRendererOutputOptions { toneMapping?; exposure?; dithering? }; interface AuraResolutionOptions { pixelRatio?: number|'device'; maxPixelRatio?; dynamic?; targetFrameMs?; minRenderScale?; allowSubCssResolution? }; AuraCreateAppRendererOptions gains quality?, output?, resolution?, msaa?: 0|2|4, compile?: { mode?: 'warm-then-block'|'async-skip' }, strictMount?, debug?: { preserveDrawingBuffer? }; mode and fallback removed (AuraMigrationError).

### PRD-02 Lighting / IBL / Reflections / Shadows

Extraction note: (docs/project/aura3d-quality-rebuild/PRD-02-lighting-ibl-reflection-shadows.md, 2049 lines, all read). Verified at HEAD on branch aura3d-quality-rebuild/audit. Line drift: createProductionRuntimeFallbackLights runs 13363 to about 13413, not 13401. createProductionRuntimeRendererInput starts at 13842; the 13990-14006 slice is the RenderSource literal and the cameraLike line. Renderer.resolveCamera is a free function at 2936. ### PRD-03 Postprocessing / AA / Tone Mapping / Cinematic Pipeline

Extraction note: (/Users/gurbakshchahal/platforms/aura3d/docs/project/aura3d-quality-rebuild/PRD-03-postprocessing-aa-tonemap-cinematic.md, 1774 lines; section 12 lists dependencies). Things I checked in the code that matter for planning: (a) `renderFrameNow()` does not exist anywhere in packages/. The sync screenshot plan (6.2) has to add it to the production controller. (b) WebGL2Device already defaults `preserveDrawingBuffer` to false (WebGL2Device.ts:354). The `true` values come from the engine profiles (index.ts:4232-4302) and canvas creation (index.ts:13591/13593). (c) There is a second hard-coded `getContext('webgl2',{antialias:true,preserveDrawingBuffer:true})` at index.ts:15989 that the PRD does not list. (d) A second `fxaaRequested` default-to-fxaa sits in the diagnostics path at index.ts:4499/4516, separate from the bridge at :12859. The PRD does not list it either. (e) None of these exist yet and all must be created: packages/rendering/src/post/, packages/rendering/src/reference/, agent-api/postBridge.ts, agent-api/postPresets.ts, tools/quality-rebuild/codemods/, apps/postprocessing-custom, .github/workflows/post-quality.yml. (f) The rendering package.json has no "./reference" export yet. (g) The PRD's line anchors otherwise check out. Exceptions: TemporalHistory's throw is at :72-74 (PRD says :73-74). The SceneOptimization instance-transform code is at :208-218, close to the PRD's :207-217. GameRenderPreset `targetFormat:"rgba8"` is at :373 and :450.

AuraResolvedMaterialInfo is { name; featureKey; baseColorFactor; enabledMaps: AuraMaterialTextureSlot[]; extensions: string[]; lightsEvaluated: 'uniform-16'|'clustered'; warnings }.
- app.diagnostics().materials: AuraMaterialDiagnostics { programs; programCompileMs; transmissionTargetActive; lightsDroppedByMaterial; paths:{materialModel,transmission,ktx2,tangents}; textureBytes; textureBudgetBytes; downscaledTextures; issues: AuraMaterialIssue[] }. Consumed by 11, 12, 14.
- createAuraApp renderer options: AuraRendererMaterialOptions { materialStrictness?: 'warn'|'strict'; materialModel?: 'legacy'|'physical-r185'; transmission?: 'auto'|'env'|'off'; alphaToCoverage?: boolean; debugView?: 'baseColor'|'normal'|'roughness'|'metallic'|'clearcoat'|'clearcoatRoughness'|'clearcoatRoughnessEffective'|'sheen'|'F0'|'tangent'|'anisotropyDirection' }
- AuraMaterialSpec additions: specularIntensity, specularColor, specular*Map, dispersion, transmissionMap, thicknessMap, alphaMode, alphaCutoff, alphaToCoverage, doubleSided, unlit, sampling?: AuraTextureSampling, slotSampling?: Partial<Record<AuraMaterialTextureSlot, AuraTextureSampling>>. Related types: type AuraTextureWrap = 'repeat'|'clamp'|'mirror'; AuraTextureSampling { wrap?; filter?: 'trilinear'|'bilinear'|'nearest'; anisotropy? }. Consumed by 10 and 13.
- TypedGLBActor additions: TypedGLBActorOptions { materialOverrides?; variant?; decoders?: GLTFDecoderSet; textureBudget?: TextureBudgetPolicy }. Methods: setMaterialOverrides(o: readonly TypedGLBActorMaterialOverride[]): void, which re-applies idempotently from the authored snapshot; setMaterialVariant(name: string|null): void; materialVariants(): readonly string[]. applyMaterialOverrides(snapshot, materials, overrides) lives in ModelMaterialOverrides.ts.

Consumed by 06, 07 and 11.
- Rendering: PhysicalMaterial base class plus PhysicalMaterialDescriptor (one CPU struct shared by scalar, textured, instanced and skinned). TexturedPBRMaterial, PBRMaterial, InstancedPBRMaterial and SkinnedLitMaterial remain as thin subclasses.
- Rendering: class TransmissionRenderTarget { constructor(device, { scale: 0.5|1; format: 'rgba16f'|'rgba8' }); resize(w,h): void; captureFrom(sceneColor: RenderTarget): void; readonly texture: Texture; readonly mipCount: number; dispose(): void }. ForwardPass pass order: opaque → transmission capture → transmissive → blend → post. Consumed by 03 and 07 (water).

Consumed by 10.

KTX2BasisTextureTranscoderOptions changes: targetFormat becomes required, colorSpace is added, transcoderUrl is added and must be local. Consumed by 05.

### PRD-08 Camera / Controls / Game Feel

Extraction note: (/Users/gurbakshchahal/platforms/aura3d/docs/project/aura3d-quality-rebuild/PRD-08-camera-controls-game-feel.md, 1512 lines). Citations checked against HEAD with rg/sed: almost every path:line matches. Exceptions: (1) AuraCameraSpec starts at index.ts:3163, not 3160. (2) @aura3d/physics Raycast.ts:87 is `sphereCastCollider(origin, radius, direction, collider, body, opts)`; there is no `sphereCast` export, so C-9 needs a world-level wrapper. (3) ScenePhysicsBridge.pullDynamic is at :69, close to the cited 68-80 and 70-78. (4) The new dirs camera/, time/, feel/, vehicle/, controls/ under packages/engine/src/agent-api, plus tools/camera-cast-codemod, tests/fixtures/camera and benchmarks/quality-rebuild/motion, do not exist yet. All existing files and tests listed for change exist. The cinematic-architecture cast (main.ts:500) is `as unknown as Record`, not `Mutable`, so the `Mutable` grep only finds the 5 game casts.

(b) The live caller of the deprecated APIs (city.block / prefabs.cityBlock) is apps/showcase-courier-rush/src/city.ts; the aura3d-materials-environments skill (packages/create-aura3d/skills/aura3d-materials-environments/SKILL.md) also mentions them. (c) fixtures/environment-corpus/hdri has 3 1k .hdr files, as the PRD says. (d) None of these exist yet: packages/rendering/src/world, packages/engine/src/agent-api/world, packages/engine/src/production-runtime/world, packages/engine/assets, tools/impostor-bake, tools/world-content-bake. (f) HeightfieldShape is at physics/src/Shape.ts:37 and toRapierHeightfieldHeights at physics-rapier/src/HeightfieldLayout.ts:12, both as cited.

The volumetric pass block is at index.ts:12809-12824. The WebGL2Device context-lost/restored listeners are at 417-443 and are registered at 444-445. The rendering index.ts webgpu re-exports are at 1167, 1168 and 1170, and 1169 is WebGPUPostProcess, which is kept. PRD 03 post passes acquire targets from the pool
- Renderer options: `RendererOptions.quality?: QualityTierSettings; batching?; frameStats?: FrameStats; postprocessGuard?: "throw"|"drop"`; error codes POSTPROCESS_PASS_CPU_ONLY, POSTPROCESS_PASS_DROPPED:<name>, EFFECT_PENDING_GPU_PASS:<name>, POINT_SHADOW_PENDING_PRD02, WGSL_PROGRAM_MISSING, WEBGPU_SYNC_READBACK_UNSUPPORTED, QUALITY_OVERRIDE_INVALID; AuraMigrationError for experimental-webgpu
- Shader contract: GLSL multi-draw feature `drawId: "multi-draw"|"uniform"` (AURA_DRAW_ID, u_drawData RGBA32F width 1024, DRAW_TEXELS 6) and `instanceColor`/`instanceEmissive` attributes a_instanceColor/a_instanceEmissive, added to PRD 01 program chunks. Tier defines SHADOW_CASCADES, SHADOW_PCF_TAPS, MAX_LIGHTS_PER_PIXEL, SOFT_PARTICLES enter program keys
- Phase 6+: `UniformLayout`: `std140Offsets(layout: UniformBlockLayout): { size; offsets }; emitGlslBlock(layout): string; emitWgslStruct(layout, kind: "uniform"|"storage"): string`; `ProgramGenerator.generate(features, { target: "wgsl" })` returns `{ vertex; fragment; bindGroupLayouts: GPUBindGroupLayoutDescriptor[]; vertexLayout: GPUVertexBufferLayout[] }`; `WebGPURenderDevice` implementing the RenderDevice contract; `backend: "auto"` selection (Ultra only)
- Budgets and gates: the per-tier budget table (§17.1), the runner gate (§17.2) and the benchmark gate (§17.3) evaluated by `tools/perf-gate/`, which writes perf-gate.json. Also benchmark stress scenes 19-draw-call-stress and 20-instancing-100k (PRD 12, PRD 14)
- Deprecations: `qualityProfile` maps to `quality` and `performanceQuality`/`setPerformanceQuality` map to overrides, each with a one-time warning. `sceneKitPerformanceBudgets` fields are renamed budgetDrawCalls/budgetP50FrameMs with kind "aura-scene-kit-budget". `collectParticleBudgetDiagnostics` renames estimatedUpdateCostMs to heuristicUpdateCostMs, adds measuredUpdateMs and drops gpuReady

- Benchmark SceneSpec extensions (benchmarks/quality-rebuild/shared/types.ts): `type ReferenceProfile = 'contract'|'showcase'`; `type SceneOwner = 'prd12'|'prd01'|'prd02'|'prd03'|'prd04'|'prd05'|'prd06'|'prd07'|'prd08'|'prd10'|'prd11'`; `type BrokenControlId = 'no-shadows'|'no-ibl'|'dpr-half'|'no-aa'|'no-tonemap'|'flat-sky'|'albedo-only'`; `type MaskId = 'object-id'|'shadow-receiver'|'sky'|'metal'|'silhouette-edge'`; `type RegionId = 'frame'|'subject'|`object:${number}`|MaskId|'scene-minus-hud'`; `interface StripSpec { frames; intervalMs; orbitDegrees }`; SceneSpec gains `owner, referenceProfile, dprs?, masks, brokenControls, strip?, primaryCriterion, primaryRegion`

New IDs use the form `<owner>-<slug>`, e.g. prd03-bloom-hdr-threshold, prd04-clearcoat-carpaint, prd06-skinned-character-walk, prd08-motion-m1..m6

- three.js showcase reference pipeline: `runThreeShowcase(spec: SceneSpec, opts: ShowcaseOptions, host: HTMLElement): Promise<ReadyPayload>` with ShowcaseOptions {toneMapping, ao, bloom, aa, shadows, contactShadows, background, anisotropy}; routed via `?engine=three&scene=<id>&profile=showcase`
- Mask passes: `renderMasks(spec, scene, camera, kinds: MaskId[]): Promise<Record<MaskId, Uint8Array>>` via `?pass=mask`; region-masked metrics for other PRDs' acceptance
- Python metrics/detectors (tools/quality-gate/metrics/metrics.py): `flip(ref,test,mask)`, `ssim(...)`, `msssim`, `lpips_alex(ref,test)`, `delta_e2000(ref,test,mask)`, `detectors(img, masks, ref_img) -> dict` covering shadowContrast, contactDarkening, highlightEnergy, roughnessResponse, textureDetail, edgeAliasing, dynamicRange, skyVariance, subjectPresence, temporalFlicker, blankOrBlack; CLI `python -m metrics run --items items.json --out metrics.json`
- Gate types (tools/quality-gate/src/types.ts): `CaptureRef`, `EvidenceEnvironment`, `CapturedItem`, `MetricId`, `MetricValue`, `CalibratedThreshold`, `CalibrationReport`, `GoldenEntry`, `GoldenManifest`, `type GateVerdict = 'pass'|'regression'|'reference-gap'|'admitted-loss'|'calibration-broken'|'non-discriminating'|'mask-misaligned'|'forbidden-capture-flag'|'blocked-runner'|'capture-failed'`, `DifferenceClass`, `GAME_VISUAL_CATEGORIES` (27), `GAME_NONVISUAL_CATEGORIES` (6), `JudgeIdentity`, `BenchmarkJudgement`, `GameJudgement`, `PanelRoundRecord`
- Entry points: `calibrate(item: CapturedItem, metrics: readonly MetricValue[][], floors: Record<MetricId,number>): CalibrationReport`; `loadGoldens(manifestPath: string): GoldenManifest`; `compareToGolden(item, golden, metrics): { verdict: GateVerdict; failures: MetricValue[] }`; `proposeGoldenUpdate(items, round: PanelRoundRecord): GoldenManifest`; `evaluateGates({ items, metrics, goldens, bar: QualityBar }): { itemVerdicts: Map<string, GateVerdict[]>; exitCode: 0|1 }`; `judgeWithPrism(packet: JudgePacket, opts: { baseUrl; apiKeyEnv: 'PRISM_API_KEY'; model: 'claude-opus-5.5' }): Promise<BenchmarkJudgement|GameJudgement>`; `appendRound(round, indexPath): void`; `trend(indexPath, itemId): { roundId; median }[]`; `buildPacket(item, seed)`; `RUBRIC_PROMPT_VERSION`; `CALIBRATION_ITEMS`

- pnpm scripts: `quality:capture` (remote screenshot loop agents can call, e.g. `quality:capture --scenes`), `quality:games`, `quality:metrics`, `quality:gate` (replaces test:visual), `quality:calibrate`, `quality:report`, `quality:classify-tools`

(g) Orbital Defense src contains only main.ts and styles.css. (h) The scripts check-art-direction.mjs and check-route-health.mjs do not exist yet (new).
